# 6.1.3 換手與鄰區問題：鄰區較強、頻繁換手、過早／過晚換手

所屬：[6.1 根因的類型](6-1.md)

## 內文

#### 是什麼、什麼時候會用到

換手與鄰區問題，指連線態（RRC_CONNECTED）的終端在小區之間移動時，因為鄰區關係或換手觸發條件不恰當，造成該換不換、太早換、換錯對象，或在兩個小區間來回切換。常見樣態有五種：鄰區漏配（目標小區不在鄰區關係表裡）、鄰區明顯比服務小區強卻遲遲沒換、乒乓換手（ping-pong）、過早／過晚換手（too early / too late handover），以及換到錯的小區（handover to wrong cell）。維運人員通常在三種情況下碰到它：換手成功率下滑的 KPI 告警、行動性穩健最佳化（Mobility Robustness Optimisation, MRO）計數器升高，以及客服轉來「走到某路口就斷線」「通話一直斷斷續續」的申訴。鄰區關係的管理與自動鄰區關係（Automatic Neighbour Relation, ANR）功能見 TS 38.300 clause 15.3.3（[TS 38.300](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)）。

#### 怎麼做／怎麼判斷

**1. 先看換手觸發條件怎麼算。** NR 連線態換手最常用的量測事件是 A3「鄰區比 SpCell 好一個 offset」（TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4，[TS 38.331](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)）：

- 進入條件（Inequality A3-1）：`Mn + Ofn + Ocn – Hys > Mp + Ofp + Ocp + Off`
- 離開條件（Inequality A3-2）：`Mn + Ofn + Ocn + Hys < Mp + Ofp + Ocp + Off`

其中 Mn、Mp 是鄰區與 SpCell 未加 offset 的量測結果；Ofn、Ofp 是量測物件層級的 `offsetMO`；Ocn、Ocp 是小區層級的 `cellIndividualOffset`（未設定時為 0）；Hys 是 `reportConfigNR` 裡該事件的 `hysteresis`；Off 是 `a3-Offset`（同條）。在 `eventA3` 裡，`a3-Offset` 的型別是 `MeasTriggerQuantityOffset`，取值 INTEGER (-30..30)，實際值為欄位值 × 0.5 dB；`Hysteresis` 是 INTEGER (0..30)，實際值也是欄位值 × 0.5 dB；`TimeToTrigger` 是 ms0～ms5120 的列舉（TS 38.331 clause 6.3.2）。UE 必須在 `timeToTrigger` 期間內、所有經過 L3 濾波的量測都滿足進入條件，才觸發量測報告（TS 38.331 clause 5.5.4.1）。

**2. 三個參數的作用方向：**

| 參數 | 調大的效果 | 調太大的風險 | 調太小的風險 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `a3-Offset`（Off） | 鄰區要更強才觸發 | 過晚換手 | 過早換手、乒乓 |
| `hysteresis`（Hys） | 進入更難、離開也更難（兩條件差 2×Hys） | 過晚換手 | 量測波動就反覆進出 |
| `timeToTrigger` | 條件要撐更久才報 | 高速時過晚換手 | 短暫衰落也觸發，乒乓 |
| `cellIndividualOffset`（Ocn） | 個別鄰區被「加分」 | 對該鄰區過早換手 | 對該鄰區過晚換手 |

**3. 用 MRO 的定義分類失敗。** TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.2 定義系統內三種連線失敗：過晚換手是 UE 在小區待了一段長時間後發生 RLF，並到另一個小區重建；過早換手是剛換手成功不久就 RLF（或換手過程中失敗），UE 回到來源小區重建；換到錯的小區則是在來源、目標以外的第三個小區重建。TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.25.1 有對應的計數器 `HO.IntraSys.TooEarly`、`HO.IntraSys.TooLate`、`HO.IntraSys.ToWrongCell`，量測物件可到 `NRCellRelation`。詳細判斷規則見 6.2.4。

**4. 搭配換手執行計數器。** `MM.HoExeInterReq`、`MM.HoExeInterSucc`（TS 28.552 clause 5.1.1.6.1.7、5.1.1.6.1.8）可依 `NRCellRelation` 統計，找出是哪一組鄰區關係出問題。

**5. 鄰區漏配與乒乓另外看。** 漏配的特徵是某個 PCI 在量測報告裡一再出現、比服務小區強，卻沒有對它發起的換手；ANR 雖可自動新增鄰區關係，但 Neighbour Detection Function 屬於實作（TS 38.300 clause 15.3.3.1）。至於系統內（NR 內）乒乓，TS 38.300 的 MRO 只對「系統間」乒乓下了定義（clause 15.5.2.4），NR 內乒乓的時間窗通常由業者自訂。

#### 實際例子

以下為虛構的一週換手統計與組態（業者「甲電信」、小區名均為虛構）：

```
來源小區 NRCellCU=N-HSZ-0451-1（統計 2026-11-16～11-22）
NRCellRelation  目標      MM.HoExeInterReq  MM.HoExeInterSucc  TooEarly  TooLate  ToWrongCell
REL-B           N-HSZ-0452-2       4,820             4,512           3        41         2
REL-C           N-HSZ-0460-1       1,990             1,918           2         3         1

measObjectNR（服務頻率）: offsetMO rsrpOffsetSSB = dB0
  cellsToAddModList: physCellId 318 (N-HSZ-0452-2)  cellIndividualOffset rsrpOffsetSSB = dB-4
                     physCellId 77  (N-HSZ-0460-1)  cellIndividualOffset rsrpOffsetSSB = dB0
reportConfigNR eventA3: a3-Offset rsrp = 6, hysteresis = 2, timeToTrigger = ms320
```

**逐步判斷：**

1. 換手成功率：REL-B 為 4,512 ÷ 4,820 ≈ 93.6%，REL-C 為 1,918 ÷ 1,990 ≈ 96.4%，問題集中在 REL-B。
2. REL-B 的 46 件 MRO 事件中，過晚換手 41 件，約占 89.1%，過早與錯誤小區很少。
3. 換算觸發門檻（RSRP，Ofn = Ofp = 0、Ocp = 0）：Off = 6 × 0.5 = 3 dB，Hys = 2 × 0.5 = 1 dB。對 REL-C（Ocn = 0），進入條件為 Mn − Mp > 3 + 1 = 4 dB；對 REL-B（Ocn = −4 dB），進入條件變成 Mn − 4 − 1 > Mp + 3，也就是鄰區要比服務小區強 8 dB 以上，還得撐過 320 ms。
4. 結論：REL-B 的負值 `cellIndividualOffset` 讓觸發條件比其他鄰區嚴格 4 dB，與「過晚換手集中在這組關係」吻合，是值得優先查證的假設。

**不能下結論的部分：** 計數器只告訴我們分類，沒有說明成因；過晚換手也可能是 N-HSZ-0451-1 往 B 方向本來就有涵蓋缺口（MRO 本身要和涵蓋問題區分，見 TS 38.300 clause 15.5.2.1，[TS 38.300](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)）。要確認，還得看這個 offset 是誰在何時設的（變更紀錄），以及 RLF 報告裡失敗前的量測結果。

#### 容易搞混的地方

- **A3 只決定 UE 何時「回報」，不是 UE 自己換手。** 依 TS 38.331 clause 5.5.4.1，事件觸發的是量測報告，要不要換、換去哪由網路決定（條件式換手 CHO 另計；[TS 38.331](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)）。
- **連線態換手與閒置態重選是兩套機制。** 這裡的 A3 參數不影響閒置態重選；閒置態重選看 TS 38.304，見 6.1.5。
- **hysteresis 與 a3-Offset 的作用不同。** Off 只加在不等式右邊，進入和離開的門檻一起平移；Hys 讓進入與離開門檻之間拉開 2 × Hys 的距離，用途是抑制來回觸發。
- **乒乓不等於過早換手。** 過早換手依 TS 38.300 的定義伴隨 RLF 或換手失敗；乒乓是換手都成功、但來回太頻繁，NR 內乒乓沒有 3GPP 統一定義。

#### 和其他小節的關係

觸發參數本身設錯（例如 `cellIndividualOffset` 誤植）屬 6.1.6；高速移動造成的換手頻繁看 6.1.5；過晚換手與涵蓋缺口要和 6.1.1 區分。用 RLF 報告分辨過早、過晚、錯誤小區的步驟在 6.2.4，補收 RLF 報告、MDT 見 6.2.3；改參數前的批准流程見 6.3.2 與第 9 章。（MRO 定義見 [TS 38.300](https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)）

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0（clause 5.5.4.1、5.5.4.4；clause 6.3.2 的 Hysteresis、TimeToTrigger、Q-OffsetRange、MeasObjectNR、ReportConfigNR）<https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/>；3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0（clause 15.3.3、15.5.2.1、15.5.2.2.2、15.5.2.4）<https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/>；3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0（clause 5.1.1.6.1.7、5.1.1.6.1.8、5.1.1.25.1）<https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/>

## 練習題（259 題）

### 補充練習題（85 題）

#### Missing neighbour relations and ANR

**情境 001**　Strong PCI reported but never handed over to

NorthLink Mobile (fictional) received complaints about dropped calls at a road junction served by cell TCH-0318-2. The optimisation team summarised 12 hours of A3 measurement reports and the neighbour relation table of the cell.

```
Source cell: NRCellCU TCH-0318-2 (PCI 214), SSB frequency 643296
Period: 2026-11-18 08:00-20:00
Summary of eventA3 measurement reports received from UEs (SS-RSRP)
Reported PCI | Reports where PCI is best neighbour | Mean (Mn - Mp) | NRCellRelation in NCRT | MM.HoPrepInterReq towards target
401          | 1,240                               | +4.6 dB        | yes (REL-401)          | 1,198
655          | 2,915                               | +7.9 dB        | no                     | 0
88           | 310                                 | +4.1 dB        | yes (REL-088)          | 296
Total eventA3 reports: 4,465
NCRT of TCH-0318-2 (export 2026-11-18 20:05): REL-401, REL-088, REL-512, REL-033
Vendor ANR auto-add of NCRs (vendor parameter, fictional name anrAutoAddNcr): disabled
Customer tickets 2026-11-18: 7 x "call drops at Wenxin/Zhongqing junction" (fictional)
```

**001.** Which finding is most consistent with the data?

1. PCI 655 is the frequent, strong best neighbour but lacks a relation and HO preparation.
2. PCI 401 is a missing neighbour, because it is reported 1,240 times.
3. Handover preparations towards PCI 655 are being rejected by the target gNB.
4. The a3-Offset of TCH-0318-2 is too large for all neighbours, so no neighbour is handed over to.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　PCI 655 is the best neighbour in 2,915 of 4,465 reports, with the largest mean margin (+7.9 dB), but the NCRT export has no relation for it and MM.HoPrepInterReq towards it is 0, which is the signature of a missing neighbour relation. Option 2 is wrong because REL-401 exists and 1,198 preparations were requested. Option 3 is wrong because a rejection requires a preparation request first, and none was sent (0). Option 4 is wrong because PCI 401 and PCI 88 both triggered preparations, so the A3 condition is being met for them.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.6.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**002.** According to TS 38.300 clause 15.3.3.1, which statement about the ANR function is correct?

1. The behaviour of the Neighbour Detection Function is fully specified in TS 38.300, so all vendors add relations in the same way.
2. The ANR function resides in the OAM system, and the gNB only executes the relations it receives.
3. Detection/removal are implementation-specific; OAM can add/delete NCRs and gets NCRT updates.
4. Neighbour Cell Relations are bidirectional, in the same way as an Xn link.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　TS 38.300 states that the ANR function resides in the gNB and manages the NCRT, that the Neighbour Detection and Neighbour Removal Functions are implementation specific, and that OAM can add and delete NCRs and is informed about NCRT changes. Option 1 contradicts "implementation specific". Option 2 is wrong because ANR resides in the gNB. Option 4 is wrong because NCRs are unidirectional while an Xn link is bidirectional.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**003.** Can the team conclude from this data that the missing relation to PCI 655 is the cause of the 7 customer call drops at the junction?

1. Yes, because PCI 655 is reported 2,915 times and the tickets are from the same day.
2. Cannot determine; failure records must link drops to PCI 655 at that place and time.
3. No, the drops are caused by PCI 401, which has the second highest number of reports.
4. Yes, because the vendor ANR auto-add is disabled.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　The data shows a missing relation and complaints on the same day, but nothing ties the complaint location or the failures to PCI 655; the RLF reports (failed cell, neighbour measurements, location) or drop records are needed. Option 1 confuses coincidence with causation. Option 3 has no supporting data. Option 4 only explains why the relation was not added automatically, not that it caused the drops.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.3; 3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.2.2 (RLF-Report-r16) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**004.** According to TS 38.300, what three things must hold for an NCR from a source cell to a target cell to exist?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　The gNB controlling the source cell knows the global and physical IDs (e.g. NR CGI and NR PCI) of the target cell, has an NCRT entry for the source cell identifying the target cell, and has the attributes of that entry defined (by OAM or set to default values).

**詳解**　TS 38.300 clause 15.3.3.1 lists exactly these three conditions (a, b, c). This is why a PCI seen in measurement reports is not enough: the global identity must also be known.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**005.** What is the first piece of information the team needs before an NRCellRelation towards PCI 655 can be created?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　The NR CGI of the cell that uses PCI 655 on SSB frequency 643296 (for example from a CGI-reading measurement by a UE or from the planning database), because an NCR needs the global identity and not only the PCI.

**詳解**　The measurement reports only carry the PCI. An NCR requires knowing the target's global and physical IDs, so the CGI must be identified first; only then can OAM (after approval) add the relation, or ANR add it if enabled.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clauses 15.3.3.1 and 15.3.3.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**情境 002**　NCRT change notifications and handover blocking

Harbor Wireless (fictional) exported the NCRT change notifications and per-relation handover counters of cell KHH-0412-1. A customer ticket reports drops on the road towards site KHH-0415.

```
NCRT change notifications, source NRCellCU KHH-0412-1, 2026-11-10 .. 2026-11-20
time              origin  action          relation  target       attributes
2026-11-10 02:14  ANR     ADD             REL-0733  KHH-0420-3   isHOAllowed=TRUE  isRemoveAllowed=TRUE
2026-11-12 03:01  ANR     REMOVE          REL-0733  KHH-0420-3   (vendor note: not reported for 48 h)
2026-11-12 09:20  OAM     ADD             REL-0733  KHH-0420-3   isHOAllowed=TRUE  isRemoveAllowed=FALSE
2026-11-15 03:00  ANR     REMOVE-SKIPPED  REL-0733  KHH-0420-3   (vendor note: isRemoveAllowed=FALSE)
2026-11-17 14:35  OAM     MODIFY          REL-0512  KHH-0415-2   isHOAllowed TRUE -> FALSE (ticket CHG-8841)

Handover execution counters, 2026-11-18 00:00 .. 2026-11-20 24:00
relation  target       MM.HoExeInterReq  MM.HoExeInterSucc
REL-0733  KHH-0420-3   1,406             1,371
REL-0512  KHH-0415-2   0                 0
REL-0619  KHH-0419-1   2,210             2,187
Customer ticket TT-7731: "calls drop on Linsen Rd towards KHH-0415" (fictional)
```

**006.** Why does REL-0512 show zero handover executions from 2026-11-18 onwards?

1. ANR removed REL-0512 because it was not reported for 48 h.
2. isRemoveAllowed was set to FALSE, which blocks handovers on the relation.
3. REL-0512 was deleted by OAM on 2026-11-17 under CHG-8841.
4. isHOAllowed became FALSE; handovers on REL-0512 are then prohibited.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　The only change to REL-0512 is the OAM modification of isHOAllowed from TRUE to FALSE on 11-17; TS 28.541 says that if isHOAllowed is FALSE, handover shall not be allowed, which matches zero executions afterwards. Option 1 is wrong because the ANR removal concerned REL-0733, not REL-0512. Option 2 confuses isRemoveAllowed (deletion protection) with isHOAllowed. Option 3 is wrong because CHG-8841 was a MODIFY of isHOAllowed, not a deletion, and REL-0512 still reports counters for 11-18 to 11-20.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.541 V18.15.0 clause 4.3.32 (NRCellRelation) and attribute definition of isHOAllowed (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.541/)

</details>

**007.** What does isRemoveAllowed = FALSE mean for an NRCellRelation according to TS 28.541?

1. The NRCellRelation instance shall not be removed (deleted) by any entity but an MnS consumer.
2. The NRCellRelation can never be removed, not even by the operator through OAM.
3. Handover over this relation is prohibited.
4. The relation is automatically mirrored in the target cell's NCRT.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　TS 28.541 defines isRemoveAllowed FALSE as "shall not be removed (deleted) by any entity but an MnS consumer", which is why ANR skipped the removal on 11-15 while OAM could still delete it. Option 2 overstates it (an MnS consumer may remove it). Option 3 describes isHOAllowed = FALSE. Option 4 is wrong because neighbour cell relations are unidirectional.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.541 V18.15.0 attribute definition of isRemoveAllowed (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.541/)

</details>

**008.** What is the handover execution success ratio of REL-0733 in the counter period?

1. 96.5%
2. 97.5%
3. 99.0%
4. 2.5%

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Ratio = MM.HoExeInterSucc / MM.HoExeInterReq = 1,371 / 1,406 = 0.9751, i.e. about 97.5%. Option 1 is not derived from the data. Option 3 is REL-0619's ratio (2,187 / 2,210 = 0.9896). Option 4 is the failure share (100% - 97.5%), not the success ratio.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**009.** Can you conclude that the isHOAllowed change on REL-0512 caused the drops in ticket TT-7731?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the data has no ticket time, no failure records on Linsen Rd and no reason for CHG-8841; RLF reports from KHH-0412-1 (failedPCellId, measResultNeighCells showing KHH-0415-2, locationInfo) for the ticket period and the change record are needed.

**詳解**　Zero executions on REL-0512 show that handovers were blocked, but not that UEs on Linsen Rd failed because of it. The ticket has no timestamp to compare with 11-17 14:35, and the change may have been made on purpose (e.g. a planned shutdown of KHH-0415-2).

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.3; 3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.2.2 (RLF-Report-r16) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**010.** What does the REMOVE-SKIPPED entry on 2026-11-15 03:00 show?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　That the ANR removal of REL-0733 was not executed because OAM had re-added the relation on 11-12 09:20 with isRemoveAllowed = FALSE, which protects it from removal by any entity other than an MnS consumer.

**詳解**　ANR removed REL-0733 once (11-12 03:01) when it was still removable; after OAM re-created it with isRemoveAllowed FALSE, the next removal attempt was skipped, and REL-0733 carried 1,406 handover requests later in the period.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.541 V18.15.0 attribute definition of isRemoveAllowed (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.541/); 3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**情境 003**　ANR trace: from unknown PCI to a new relation

Telco-X (fictional) captured a gNB trace of the ANR procedure in cell HSZ-7731-1. PLMN 001-01 is a test PLMN identity used in this example.

```
gNB GNB-7731, cell HSZ-7731-1 (PCI 140), trace 2026-11-21
10:02:11.120  UE#88  MeasurementReport measId=3 (eventA3): neighbour PCI 903 RSRP -98 dBm; serving PCI 140 RSRP -104 dBm
10:02:11.135  gNB    PCI 903 not found in NCRT of HSZ-7731-1
10:02:11.150  gNB    RRCReconfiguration to UE#88: reportConfig reportType=reportCGI, cellForWhichToReportCGI=903
10:02:12.410  UE#88  MeasurementReport: cgi-Info for PCI 903: PLMN 001-01, NCI 0x0B1C20413, TAC 0x002A1
10:02:12.430  gNB    NCRT add NRCellRelation REL-903 -> target HSZ-7790-3 (NCI 0x0B1C20413)
10:02:12.455  gNB    notification to OAM: NCRT changed (REL-903 created)
10:02:12.470  gNB    XnAP XN SETUP REQUEST to GNB-7790
10:02:17.470  gNB    Xn setup towards GNB-7790: no response, retry scheduled
10:05:40.010  UE#91  MeasurementReport measId=3 (eventA3): PCI 903 RSRP -97 dBm; serving PCI 140 RSRP -105 dBm
10:05:40.030  gNB    target REL-903; Xn to GNB-7790 not available -> NGAP HANDOVER REQUIRED to AMF
10:05:40.410  gNB    NGAP HANDOVER COMMAND received from AMF
10:05:40.425  gNB    RRCReconfiguration (reconfigurationWithSync) sent to UE#91
(end of excerpt)
```

**011.** Which step of the intra-system ANR sequence in TS 38.300 does the entry at 10:02:11.150 correspond to?

1. The Neighbour Removal Function deleting an outdated NCR.
2. Setting up a new Xn interface towards the neighbour gNB.
3. Step 2: the gNB asks the UE to read the detected cell's NCGI and related identities.
4. OAM adding the relation manually through the NCRT management interface.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　After a measurement report with an unknown PCI, TS 38.300 step 2 has the NG-RAN node instruct the UE, using that PCI as a parameter, to read the broadcast NCGI(s), TAC(s) and other identities; the reportCGI configuration with cellForWhichToReportCGI=903 is that step. Option 1 is a removal, not shown here. Option 2 happens later (10:02:12.470). Option 4 is wrong because the addition at 10:02:12.430 is made by the gNB, and OAM is only notified.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.3.1 (reportCGI) and clause 6.3.2 (ReportCGI) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**012.** Why can the gNB not create the NCR directly from the first measurement report at 10:02:11.120?

1. Because the neighbour RSRP of -98 dBm is below the ANR threshold.
2. Because A3 reports may only be used for handover decisions, not for ANR.
3. Because every new NCR requires prior OAM approval.
4. No NCGI yet; the first report has PCI only.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　TS 38.300 states that the measurement report regarding cell B contains its PCI but not its NCGI, and an existing NCR requires the gNB to know the target's global and physical IDs; therefore the CGI must be read first. Option 1 invents a threshold. Option 2 is wrong because ANR explicitly uses UE measurement reports. Option 3 is wrong because the gNB may add the relation itself and only informs OAM.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clauses 15.3.3.1 and 15.3.3.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**013.** Why was the handover of UE#91 at 10:05:40 prepared through the AMF?

1. Xn setup was pending, so preparation used NGAP via AMF.
2. Because REL-903 was missing from the NCRT at that time.
3. Because PCI 903 belongs to the same gNB, so an intra-gNB handover was used.
4. Because isHOAllowed was FALSE for REL-903.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　The trace shows REL-903 added at 10:02:12.430 and the Xn setup unanswered at 10:02:17.470; at 10:05:40.030 the gNB notes that Xn is not available and sends HANDOVER REQUIRED to the AMF. An NCR is a cell-to-cell relation and is separate from the Xn link between gNBs. Option 2 contradicts the 10:02:12.430 entry. Option 3 is wrong because PCI 903 belongs to GNB-7790. Option 4 would block the handover altogether, but a preparation was made.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.6.1.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**014.** Did the handover of UE#91 to HSZ-7790-3 succeed?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the excerpt ends after the RRCReconfiguration was sent; the UE CONTEXT RELEASE COMMAND from the AMF following a successful handover (the trigger of MM.HoExeInterSucc for NG-based handover) or the target-side completion is needed.

**詳解**　HANDOVER COMMAND only shows that preparation succeeded, and the RRCReconfiguration shows that execution was attempted (the trigger of MM.HoExeInterReq). Success of the execution is not visible in the trace.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.2, 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**015.** Does the creation of REL-903 in HSZ-7731-1 also allow UEs served by HSZ-7790-3 to be handed over to HSZ-7731-1?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　No; Neighbour Cell Relations are unidirectional (unlike the bidirectional Xn link), so the reverse relation must exist in the NCRT of HSZ-7790-3.

**詳解**　TS 38.300 states that NCRs are cell-to-cell and unidirectional, while an Xn link is set up between two gNBs and is bidirectional; TS 28.541 also describes NRCellRelation as unidirectional.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 28.541 V18.15.0 clause 4.3.32.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.541/)

</details>

#### Neighbour stronger than the serving cell but no handover

**情境 004**　Periodic measurements with a negative cell individual offset

NorthLink Mobile (fictional) traced a UE in cell CHY-2207-1 whose user complained that the call quality fell while a neighbour was clearly stronger. Periodic reports (every 500 ms) were configured in addition to the A3 report configuration below.

```
Cell CHY-2207-1 (PCI 52), UE trace 2026-11-19, SS-RSRP in dBm (L3-filtered, periodic reports)
measObjectNR ssbFrequency 627264: offsetMO rsrpOffsetSSB = dB0
  cellsToAddModList: physCellId 610  cellIndividualOffset rsrpOffsetSSB = dB-6
                     physCellId 37   cellIndividualOffset rsrpOffsetSSB = dB2
reportConfigNR (reportConfigId 2) eventA3: a3-Offset rsrp = 4, hysteresis = 2,
  timeToTrigger = ms640, reportOnLeave = false
Serving cell: no cellIndividualOffset configured for PCI 52

time          PCI 52   PCI 610   PCI 37
17:40:00.0    -103     -97       -108
17:40:00.5    -104     -97       -109
17:40:01.0    -104     -98       -110
17:40:01.5    -105     -98       -109
17:40:02.0    -106     -98       -111
17:40:02.5    -107     -100      -112
17:40:03.0    -108     -101      -113
17:40:03.5    -109     -102      -113
eventA3 measurement reports sent by the UE in this interval: none
```

**016.** For PCI 610, how much stronger than PCI 52 (Mn - Mp, unoffset SS-RSRP) must it be to fulfil the A3 entering condition with this configuration?

1. More than 3 dB
2. More than 9 dB
3. More than 8 dB
4. More than 12 dB

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　A3-1: Mn + Ofn + Ocn - Hys > Mp + Ofp + Ocp + Off. Off = 4 x 0.5 = 2 dB; Hys = 2 x 0.5 = 1 dB; Ocn = -6 dB; Ofn = Ofp = 0; Ocp = 0 (not configured). So Mn - 6 - 1 > Mp + 2, i.e. Mn - Mp > 2 + 1 + 6 = 9 dB. Option 1 ignores the -6 dB offset (2 + 1 = 3). Option 3 forgets the hysteresis (2 + 6 = 8). Option 4 uses the raw field values (4 + 2 + 6 = 12) instead of field value x 0.5 dB.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 and clause 6.3.2 (Hysteresis; ReportConfigNR field description a3-Offset) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**017.** In which rows of the trace is the A3 entering condition fulfilled for PCI 610?

1. In all rows, because PCI 610 is always stronger than PCI 52.
2. In the rows where PCI 610 is at least 7 dB stronger.
3. Only in the last row (17:40:03.5).
4. None; A3 requires >9 dB and the maximum is 8 dB.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　Margins Mn - Mp per row: 6, 7, 6, 7, 8, 7, 7, 7 dB. The required margin is more than 9 dB, so no row satisfies A3-1, which matches "no eventA3 measurement reports". Option 1 ignores the offsets. Option 2 uses the wrong threshold. Option 3 is wrong because the last row has 7 dB (-102 vs -109).

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clauses 5.5.4.1 and 5.5.4.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**018.** According to TS 38.331 clause 5.5.4.4, what value is used for Ocn if no cellIndividualOffset is configured for the neighbour cell?

1. Zero
2. The a3-Offset of the event
3. The offsetMO of the neighbour's frequency
4. No value: the cell is not applicable for event A3

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　TS 38.331 defines Ocn as the cell specific offset of the neighbour cell, "set to zero if not configured for the neighbour cell". Option 2 confuses Ocn with Off. Option 3 confuses Ocn with Ofn. Option 4 is wrong because applicability of a detected cell depends on the excluded/allowed cell lists, not on whether a CIO is configured.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**019.** In the A3 inequalities, which cell do Mp, Ofp and Ocp refer to?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　The SpCell (its unoffset measurement result, the offsetMO of its measurement object and its own cellIndividualOffset).

**詳解**　TS 38.331 clause 5.5.4.4 says the UE shall "use the SpCell for Mp, Ofp and Ocp"; the event is "Neighbour becomes offset better than SpCell".

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**020.** Is the dB-6 cellIndividualOffset for PCI 610 a configuration error?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined from this data: the planned value for PCI 610 and the change record (who set dB-6, when and why) are needed, because a negative offset may be intentional, for example to keep traffic off that cell.

**詳解**　The trace proves that the offset prevents A3 from triggering, but whether that is a mistake depends on the plan; comparing configuration with planned values belongs to 6.1.6.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.3 and 6.1.6; 3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**情境 005**　Reports arrive, preparations fail

Telco-X (fictional) investigates cell TNN-0901-1, where field engineers see a stronger neighbour but UEs stay on the serving cell. The table shows one day of counters per NRCellRelation; the measurement-report column is a vendor counter.

| NRCellRelation | Target | A3 reports received (vendor counter) | MM.HoPrepInterReq | MM.HoPrepInterSucc | MM.HoPrepInterFail (sum of causes) | MM.HoExeInterReq | MM.HoExeInterSucc |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| REL-11 | TNN-0905-2 | 3,480 | 3,402 | 1,020 | 2,382 | 1,020 | 1,001 |
| REL-12 | TNN-0906-1 | 1,150 | 1,121 | 1,118 | 3 | 1,118 | 1,097 |
| Period | 2026-11-24 00:00-24:00 | | | | | | |
| Note | both relations: isHOAllowed = TRUE; Xn link to both target gNBs = up | | | | | | |

**021.** Where does the handover chain for REL-11 break down?

1. The neighbour relation is missing, so no handover is attempted.
2. Event A3 does not trigger, so the gNB receives no measurement reports.
3. Preparation: only 30% succeed, versus 98% execution success.
4. In handover execution on the radio interface.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　Reports (3,480) and preparation requests (3,402) are plentiful, but preparation success is 1,020 / 3,402 = 0.2998 (about 30.0%), while execution success is 1,001 / 1,020 = 0.9814 (about 98.1%). The bottleneck is the preparation phase. Option 1 is wrong because REL-11 exists and preparations are requested. Option 2 is wrong because 3,480 reports were received. Option 4 is wrong because execution success is about 98%.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.1 to 5.1.1.6.1.3, 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**022.** What is the handover preparation success ratio of REL-11?

1. 70.0%
2. 30.0%
3. 98.1%
4. 29.3%

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　MM.HoPrepInterSucc / MM.HoPrepInterReq = 1,020 / 3,402 = 0.2998, about 30.0%. Option 1 is the failure ratio (2,382 / 3,402 = 0.7002). Option 3 is the execution success ratio (1,001 / 1,020). Option 4 divides by the vendor report counter (1,020 / 3,480 = 0.2931), which is not the preparation request count.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.1 and 5.1.1.6.1.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**023.** On which event is MM.HoPrepInterFail.cause incremented?

1. On receipt of HANDOVER PREPARATION FAILURE, counted by cause.
2. On reception of an RRCReestablishmentRequest with cause handoverFailure in the source gNB.
3. On expiry of the handover execution supervision timer in the source gNB.
4. On receipt of UE CONTEXT RELEASE from the target gNB.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　TS 28.552 clause 5.1.1.6.1.3 counts each received HANDOVER PREPARATION FAILURE (TS 38.413 or TS 38.423) per failure cause. Options 2 and 3 are events of MM.HoExeInterFail (execution failures). Option 4 is the trigger of MM.HoExeInterSucc.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.3, 5.1.1.6.1.8 and 5.1.1.6.1.9 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**024.** Is the target cell TNN-0905-2 overloaded?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the table gives only the sum of preparation failures; the per-cause subcounters MM.HoPrepInterFail.cause and load data of TNN-0905-2 (for example PRB usage and RRC connection counts) are needed.

**詳解**　Preparation failures can have many causes (resources, target not allowed, unknown target and others); only the cause breakdown and the target's load show whether overload is the reason.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.6.1.3 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**025.** What happens to the UEs whose REL-11 preparation fails, and what MRO class could result?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　They stay on TNN-0901-1 although TNN-0905-2 is reported stronger, so radio conditions keep degrading; if an RLF follows and the UE re-establishes in another cell after a long stay, MRO counts it as an intra-system too late handover.

**詳解**　A failed preparation means no handover command is sent; the UE remains in the serving cell. TS 38.300 defines too late handover as RLF after a long stay in the cell with re-establishment in a different cell.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

#### A3 offset, hysteresis and time-to-trigger in practice

**情境 007**　Change request to retune event A3

Telco-X (fictional) received a change request from an optimisation engineer. All values are the field values as written in reportConfigNR and measObjectNR (RSRP based).

```
Change request CR-2026-1187, cluster TYN-N05, reportConfigNR eventA3 (rsrp)
Field                                   Current    Proposed
a3-Offset                               2          6
hysteresis                              4          2
timeToTrigger                           ms160      ms320
reportOnLeave                           false      false
cellIndividualOffset (all neighbours)   dB0        dB0
offsetMO                                dB0        dB0
Author's rationale: "reduce ping-pong between TYN-0511-1 and TYN-0512-2"

Evidence attached (2026-11-16 .. 11-22, relation TYN-0511-1 -> TYN-0512-2):
  MM.HoExeInterSucc                     9,200
  HO.IntraSys.TooEarly                  12
  HO.IntraSys.TooLate                   4
  HO.IntraSys.ToWrongCell               1
  ping-pong returns (fictional operator rule: back to the
  previous cell within 5 s)             1,840
```

**031.** With the proposed values, what is the A3 entering condition expressed as Mn - Mp?

1. Mn - Mp > 8 dB
2. Mn - Mp > 3 dB
3. Mn - Mp > 4 dB
4. Mn - Mp > 2 dB

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　All offsets are 0 dB, so A3-1 reduces to Mn - Hys > Mp + Off. Proposed Off = 6 x 0.5 = 3 dB, Hys = 2 x 0.5 = 1 dB, so Mn - Mp > 3 + 1 = 4 dB. Option 1 adds the raw field values (6 + 2). Option 2 is the current entering condition (1 + 2 = 3 dB). Option 4 is the proposed leaving threshold (Mn - Mp < 3 - 1 = 2 dB), not the entering one.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 and clause 6.3.2 (Hysteresis; ReportConfigNR field description a3-Offset) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**032.** How does the gap between the entering and leaving thresholds change with the proposal?

1. The gap halves from 4 dB to 2 dB.
2. It stays at 4 dB, because only the offset moves.
3. It widens from 2 dB to 4 dB.
4. The gap equals the a3-Offset, so it widens from 1 dB to 3 dB.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　Entering: Mn - Mp > Off + Hys; leaving: Mn - Mp < Off - Hys; gap = 2 x Hys. Current: Hys = 4 x 0.5 = 2 dB, gap 4 dB (entering > 3 dB, leaving < -1 dB). Proposed: Hys = 1 dB, gap 2 dB (entering > 4 dB, leaving < 2 dB). The proposal therefore narrows the band that suppresses repeated triggering, which does not fit the stated aim. Option 2 ignores the hysteresis change. Option 3 reverses the direction. Option 4 confuses the offset (which shifts both thresholds) with the gap.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/); Curriculum 6.1.3

</details>

**033.** What range and step can the hysteresis of event A3 take in NR?

1. 0 to 30 dB in 1 dB steps
2. 0 to 15 dB in 1 dB steps
3. -15 to +15 dB in 0.5 dB steps
4. 0–15 dB, in 0.5-dB steps.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　TS 38.331 defines Hysteresis ::= INTEGER (0..30) and "the actual value is field value * 0.5 dB", so 0 to 15 dB in 0.5 dB steps. Option 1 takes the field value as dB. Option 2 has the right span but the wrong step. Option 3 is the range of a3-Offset (INTEGER -30..30 x 0.5 dB), not of hysteresis.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.3.2 (Hysteresis) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**034.** Why does increasing hysteresis suppress repeated triggering, while increasing a3-Offset does not?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Hys is subtracted in the entering condition and added in the leaving condition, so it opens a band of 2 x Hys between them, whereas Off appears identically in both conditions and only shifts the two thresholds together.

**詳解**　A3-1: Mn + Ofn + Ocn - Hys > Mp + Ofp + Ocp + Off; A3-2: Mn + Ofn + Ocn + Hys < Mp + Ofp + Ocp + Off. Measurement fluctuations smaller than the band cannot move the cell in and out of the triggered state.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**035.** Will the proposal bring the ping-pong returns of this pair below 5% of successful handovers?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the request contains only counters; the distribution of Mn - Mp (measurement reports or traces) for UEs on this pair and the dwell time before each return are needed to estimate how many returns the new thresholds would remove.

**詳解**　Today 1,840 / 9,200 = 20% of handovers are returns under the operator rule. Whether a 4 dB entering threshold, a 2 dB band and 320 ms TTT remove enough of them depends on the radio margins and timing, which are not in the data.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.3; 3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.4 (only inter-system ping-pong is defined) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**情境 008**　Time-to-trigger on a sample timeline

A UE trace from cell TXG-3302-1 shows L3-filtered SS-RSRP every 80 ms. For this exercise, assume the entering condition is evaluated only on these samples.

```
UE trace, serving PCI 233 (cell TXG-3302-1), neighbour PCI 905, Telco-X (fictional), 2026-11-24
reportConfigNR eventA3: a3-Offset rsrp = 6, hysteresis = 1, timeToTrigger = ms320
offsetMO = dB0, no cellIndividualOffset for PCI 233 or PCI 905
t (ms)   Mp (PCI 233)   Mn (PCI 905)
0        -101.0         -97.0
80       -101.5         -97.0
160      -102.0         -97.5
240      -102.0         -98.5
320      -102.5         -98.5
400      -103.0         -98.5
480      -103.5         -98.5
560      -103.5         -99.5
640      -104.0         -98.5
720      -104.5         -98.5
800      -105.0         -98.5
880      -105.0         -98.0
```

**036.** What is the entering threshold for Mn - Mp in this configuration?

1. 7 dB
2. 3.5 dB
3. 3.0 dB
4. 4.0 dB

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Off = 6 x 0.5 = 3 dB, Hys = 1 x 0.5 = 0.5 dB, all other offsets 0; A3-1 gives Mn - Mp > 3 + 0.5 = 3.5 dB. Option 1 adds raw field values (6 + 1). Option 3 ignores the hysteresis. Option 4 treats the hysteresis field value 1 as 1 dB.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 and clause 6.3.2 (Hysteresis; ReportConfigNR field description a3-Offset) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**037.** Under the stated assumption, at what time is the measurement report first triggered?

1. 320 ms
2. 240 ms
3. 640 ms
4. 720 ms

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　Margins Mn - Mp: 4.0, 4.5, 4.5, 3.5, 4.0, 4.5, 5.0, 4.0, 5.5, 6.0, 6.5, 7.0 dB. The sample at 240 ms (3.5 dB) does not exceed 3.5 dB, so the condition must hold again from 320 ms; all samples from 320 to 640 ms (a span of 320 ms) satisfy it, so the report is triggered at 640 ms. Option 1 ignores the break at 240 ms. Option 2 is the failing sample itself. Option 4 starts the window one sample too late (at 400 ms).

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.1 (entry condition fulfilled for all measurements after layer 3 filtering taken during timeToTrigger) and clause 6.3.2 (TimeToTrigger) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**038.** At t = 240 ms the margin is exactly 3.5 dB. What does this mean for event A3?

1. The entering condition is fulfilled, because the threshold is reached.
2. The leaving condition is fulfilled.
3. The time-to-trigger pauses and continues at the next fulfilling sample.
4. No; A3-1 requires a margin strictly greater than 3.5 dB.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　A3-1 uses ">" so 3.5 dB is not enough; because the entering condition must hold for all L3-filtered measurements during timeToTrigger, the window restarts. Option 1 treats ">" as ">=". Option 2 is wrong because leaving needs Mn - Mp < 3 - 0.5 = 2.5 dB. Option 3 is wrong because the entering condition must be fulfilled for all measurements taken during timeToTrigger, so a failing sample cannot be skipped.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clauses 5.5.4.1 and 5.5.4.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**039.** When the A3 report is triggered at the UE, is the UE thereby handed over to PCI 905?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　No; the event only triggers a measurement report, and the network decides whether and where to hand the UE over (conditional handover is a separate case).

**詳解**　TS 38.331 clause 5.5.4.1 says that when the entry condition is fulfilled during timeToTrigger the UE initiates the measurement reporting procedure; the handover itself is ordered by the network.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**040.** An engineer suspects that layer 3 filtering is smoothing the RSRP too much for this UE. Can this be checked from the trace?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the trace shows only filtered values; the configured L3 filter coefficient (quantityConfig) and unfiltered (layer 1) samples are needed.

**詳解**　Without the filter configuration and the raw samples, the effect of filtering on the timeline cannot be assessed.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.3.2 (layer 3 filtering) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**情境 009**　Three clusters, three parameter sets

Telco-X (fictional) compares one week of KPIs from three clusters that use different event A3 settings (field values, RSRP, all offsets dB0).

| Cluster | a3-Offset | hysteresis | timeToTrigger | Handover executions per day (mean) | HO.IntraSys.TooEarly (week) | HO.IntraSys.TooLate (week) | Ping-pong returns, % of handovers (fictional operator rule: return within 5 s) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A (dense urban) | 2 | 1 | ms40 | 18,400 | 61 | 9 | 14.2% |
| B (suburban) | 6 | 2 | ms320 | 9,950 | 7 | 15 | 3.1% |
| C (highway) | 10 | 4 | ms1280 | 5,120 | 2 | 88 | 0.9% |
| Period | 2026-11-16 .. 11-22 | | | | | | |

**041.** Which cluster shows the signature of handover triggering that is set too late?

1. Cluster C
2. Cluster A
3. Cluster B
4. None of the clusters

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　Cluster C has the strictest settings (entering at Mn - Mp > 5 + 2 = 7 dB and 1,280 ms TTT) and by far the most too late handovers (88) with almost no too early ones (2) or returns (0.9%). Option 2 shows the opposite pattern (too early 61, returns 14.2%). Option 3 is balanced. Option 4 ignores cluster C's counters.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.25.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**042.** Which statement about the IE TimeToTrigger in TS 38.331 is correct?

1. An integer count of milliseconds between 0 and 5120.
2. A fixed set of values with ms2560 as its upper limit.
3. Sixteen enumerated values spanning ms0 through ms5120.
4. An interval from 0 to 5.12 seconds in 0.04-second steps.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　TimeToTrigger ::= ENUMERATED {ms0, ms40, ms64, ms80, ms100, ms128, ms160, ms256, ms320, ms480, ms512, ms640, ms1024, ms1280, ms2560, ms5120}; value ms0 corresponds to 0 ms with the behaviour in 7.1.2. Option 1 is wrong because arbitrary integers are not allowed. Option 2 omits ms5120. Option 4 invents a uniform step.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.3.2 (TimeToTrigger) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**043.** What is the entering threshold (Mn - Mp) in cluster C?

1. 5 dB
2. 7 dB
3. 14 dB
4. 9 dB

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Off = 10 x 0.5 = 5 dB, Hys = 4 x 0.5 = 2 dB; entering: Mn - Mp > 5 + 2 = 7 dB. Option 1 ignores hysteresis. Option 3 adds the raw field values (10 + 4). Option 4 adds twice the hysteresis (5 + 2 x 2), confusing the width of the hysteresis band with the entering threshold.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 and clause 6.3.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**044.** Can you conclude that cluster A's high ping-pong rate is caused by its A3 parameters rather than by its environment?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the clusters differ in environment (site density, UE speed, traffic), so the comparison is confounded; a before/after comparison on the same cluster (or a controlled trial on part of it) is needed.

**詳解**　The table shows correlation across different areas only. Dense urban cells overlap more, which can produce returns regardless of parameters.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.3 and 6.2.2

</details>

**045.** What range and step can a3-Offset take?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　-15 dB to +15 dB in 0.5 dB steps (MeasTriggerQuantityOffset INTEGER -30..30, actual value = field value x 0.5 dB).

**詳解**　In eventA3, a3-Offset is of type MeasTriggerQuantityOffset, a CHOICE of rsrp, rsrq or sinr, each INTEGER (-30..30); the field description gives the actual value as field value x 0.5 dB.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.3.2 (ReportConfigNR, MeasTriggerQuantityOffset) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

#### Ping-pong handovers

**情境 011**　Inter-system ping-pong counters

Telco-X (fictional) reviews one week of inter-system MRO counters of an NR cell towards its E-UTRA neighbours.

```
NRCellCU TPE-5520-1, week 2026-11-09 .. 11-15, measured per EutranRelation
Relation  Target E-UTRA cell  MM.HoOutExe5gsToEpsSucc  HO.InterSys.PingPong  HO.InterSys.Unnecessary  HO.InterSys.TooLate
ER-01     L-TPE-3301-1        1,850                    37                    12                       9
ER-02     L-TPE-3302-2        2,460                    418                   95                       4
ER-03     L-TPE-3315-1        640                      22                    3                        31
Cell level: MM.HoOutExe5gsToEpsFallbackSucc (EPS fallback) = 2,912
NR -> E-UTRA trigger (fictional operator setting): event B2,
  b2-Threshold1 (NR serving) SS-RSRP < -112 dBm, b2-Threshold2EUTRA RSRP > -110 dBm
```

**051.** Which relation has the highest ratio of inter-system ping-pong events to successful 5GS-to-EPS handover executions?

1. ER-01, about 2.0%
2. ER-03, about 3.4%
3. ER-02, about 17.0%
4. ER-03, because it has the most too late handovers

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　ER-01: 37 / 1,850 = 0.0200 (2.0%); ER-02: 418 / 2,460 = 0.1699 (17.0%); ER-03: 22 / 640 = 0.0344 (3.4%). ER-02 is highest. Options 1 and 2 are the lower ratios. Option 4 answers a different question (too late, not ping-pong).

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.3.8 and 5.1.1.25.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**052.** According to TS 38.300 clause 15.5.2.4, which of the following is not considered inter-system ping-pong?

1. NR to E-UTRA handover followed within a predefined limited time by a handover back to NR while NR coverage was sufficient.
2. The same back-and-forth sequence occurring more than once for one UE.
3. A ping-pong case where the source NG-RAN node of the first handover differs from the target NG-RAN node of the second one.
4. Inter-system mobility triggered by voice fallback.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　The NOTE in clause 15.5.2.4 says inter-system mobility triggered by voice fallback is not considered inter-system ping-pong, which is also why the EPS fallback count is not part of the ping-pong counters. Option 1 is the definition itself. Option 2 is covered ("the event may occur more than once"). Option 3 is a case the clause handles (the target node may report it to the source node).

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**053.** What is the appropriate next step for ER-02 before changing the NR-to-E-UTRA thresholds?

1. Lower b2-Threshold1 to -120 dBm immediately on all relations.
2. Verify NR coverage during ping-pong before changing thresholds.
3. Increase the hysteresis of the intra-NR event A3 to suppress the ping-pong.
4. Delete relation ER-02 so that UEs cannot go to L-TPE-3302-2.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　TS 38.300 clause 15.5.2.4 describes the solution as collecting ping-pong statistics and then performing coverage verification to check whether the mobility to the other system was inevitable, using the Unnecessary HO procedure if needed. Option 1 changes parameters network-wide without verification. Option 3 targets intra-NR A3, which does not control NR-to-E-UTRA handover. Option 4 removes mobility and could cause too late handovers or drops.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clauses 15.5.2.3 and 15.5.2.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**054.** Can the intra-NR ping-pong rate of TPE-5520-1 be derived from this table?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the table contains only inter-system counters; intra-NR ping-pong has no TS 28.552 measurement, so per-UE intra-NR handover sequences evaluated with an operator-defined time window are needed.

**詳解**　HO.InterSys.PingPong covers NG-RAN and E-UTRAN only (measured per EutranRelation). Intra-NR returns must be counted from handover records under an operator rule.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.25.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); Curriculum 6.1.3

</details>

**055.** What does the combination of 418 ping-pong events and 95 unnecessary handovers on ER-02 suggest about the NR serving threshold for leaving to E-UTRA?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　That the NR serving-cell threshold (here b2-Threshold1 at -112 dBm) may be set too high, so UEs leave NR although NR coverage was still sufficient; this remains a hypothesis until coverage is verified.

**詳解**　TS 38.300 clause 15.5.2.3 explains that if the serving NR threshold is set too high while a good E-UTRA cell is available, an inter-system handover may be triggered unnecessarily; both counters describe handovers made while NR coverage was sufficient (95 / 2,460 = 3.9% unnecessary, 17.0% ping-pong).

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.3 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.25.3 and 5.1.1.25.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**情境 012**　Mutual positive offsets after an offload change

Telco-X (fictional) changed the cell individual offsets between two neighbouring cells to move traffic. A week later the operator's ping-pong count for the pair rose sharply.

```
Configuration export 2026-11-22
Cell TNN-1101-1 (PCI 420): eventA3 a3-Offset rsrp = 2, hysteresis = 1, timeToTrigger = ms128
   measObjectNR: offsetMO dB0; cellIndividualOffset rsrpOffsetSSB for PCI 421 = dB3; own CIO not configured
Cell TNN-1102-1 (PCI 421): eventA3 a3-Offset rsrp = 2, hysteresis = 1, timeToTrigger = ms128
   measObjectNR: offsetMO dB0; cellIndividualOffset rsrpOffsetSSB for PCI 420 = dB3; own CIO not configured
Change history: 2026-11-14 CHG-9017 cellIndividualOffset dB0 -> dB3 on both cells
   (reason in ticket: "help offload to neighbour")

Pair PCI 420 <-> PCI 421, ping-pong returns (fictional operator rule: return within 5 s)
week            successful HO (both directions)  ping-pong returns  HO.IntraSys.TooEarly  HO.IntraSys.TooLate
11-03 .. 11-09  6,210                            248                14                    22
11-10 .. 11-16  7,480                            1,402              17                    13
11-17 .. 11-23  8,940                            2,861              19                    5
```

**056.** For a UE served by TNN-1101-1, what is the A3 entering condition towards PCI 421, expressed as M421 - M420?

1. M421 - M420 > -1.5 dB
2. M421 - M420 > 1.5 dB
3. M421 - M420 > 4.5 dB
4. M421 - M420 > -2.5 dB

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　Off = 2 x 0.5 = 1 dB, Hys = 1 x 0.5 = 0.5 dB, Ocn = +3 dB, Ofn = Ofp = Ocp = 0. A3-1: M421 + 3 - 0.5 > M420 + 1, so M421 - M420 > 1 + 0.5 - 3 = -1.5 dB: the neighbour triggers even when it is 1.5 dB weaker. Option 2 ignores the CIO. Option 3 applies the CIO with the wrong sign. Option 4 subtracts the hysteresis instead of adding it.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 and clause 6.3.2 (Q-OffsetRange, Hysteresis) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**057.** The cellIndividualOffset for PCI 421 is configured in the measObjectNR of TNN-1101-1. Which handovers does it influence?

1. Both directions due to symmetric neighbour relations.
2. Only idle reselection from TNN-1101-1 to TNN-1102-1.
3. Only handovers from cell TNN-1102-1 towards TNN-1101-1.
4. A3 handover evaluation from TNN-1101-1 to TNN-1102-1.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　Ocn is the cellIndividualOffset defined within the measObjectNR that the serving cell configures for its UEs, so it affects reports from UEs in TNN-1101-1 only; here both cells added +3 dB for each other, which is why the effect is mutual. Option 1 is wrong because relations and measurement configurations are per source cell (NCRs are unidirectional). Option 2 confuses connected-mode measurement configuration with idle-mode reselection. Option 3 reverses the direction.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/); 3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**058.** What share of successful handovers were ping-pong returns in the week 11-17 .. 11-23?

1. 4.0%
2. 18.7%
3. 32.0%
4. 47.1%

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　2,861 / 8,940 = 0.3200, i.e. 32.0%. Option 1 is the week before the change (248 / 6,210 = 0.0399). Option 2 is the week of the change (1,402 / 7,480 = 0.1874). Option 4 divides by the non-return handovers (2,861 / (8,940 - 2,861) = 2,861 / 6,079 = 0.4706), which is the wrong denominator.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.3 (fictional operator rule; NR intra-system ping-pong has no unified 3GPP definition, see 3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.4, https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**059.** Did CHG-9017 achieve its goal of offloading traffic?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: handover and ping-pong counts do not show load; traffic and load measurements of both cells before and after 11-14 (for example PRB usage and UE counts) are needed.

**詳解**　More handovers in both directions can even mean no net offload, because the UEs keep returning. Only load data can show whether traffic moved.

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.3 and 6.1.4; 3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.2 (radio resource utilization) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**060.** What is the general effect of increasing timeToTrigger on ping-pong, and what is its risk?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　A longer timeToTrigger requires the entering condition to hold longer, which filters out short fades and reduces ping-pong, but it risks too late handovers, especially for fast-moving UEs.

**詳解**　The entering condition must be fulfilled for all L3-filtered measurements taken during timeToTrigger; a longer window delays every report.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/); Curriculum 6.1.3

</details>

#### Too early, too late and wrong-cell handover from RLF reports

**情境 013**　Classifying decoded RLF reports

NorthLink Mobile (fictional) fetched six NR RLF reports involving three cells. The operator has configured Tstore_UE_cntxt = 5 s (fictional operator setting); the field timeConnFailure is shown as the raw field value.

```
Cells: A = TCH-0901-1 (PCI 150), B = TCH-0902-3 (PCI 151), C = TCH-0910-2 (PCI 390)
Tstore_UE_cntxt = 5 s (fictional operator setting)

Rep  connectionFailureType  failedPCellId  previousPCellId  timeConnFailure  reestablishmentCellId  reconnectCellId / timeUntilReconnection  rlf-Cause
R1   rlf                    A              (absent)         (absent)         B                      (absent)                                 t310-Expiry
R2   rlf                    B              A                12               A                      (absent)                                 t310-Expiry
R3   hof                    B              A                3                A                      (absent)                                 randomAccessProblem
R4   rlf                    B              A                25               C                      (absent)                                 rlc-MaxNumRetx
R5   rlf                    B              A                400              C                      (absent)                                 t310-Expiry
R6   rlf                    A              (absent)         (absent)         A (attempt failed)     A / 30 s                                 t310-Expiry
```

**061.** How is R4 classified under the TS 38.300 intra-system MRO definitions?

1. Too early handover
2. Handover to wrong cell
3. Too late handover
4. Ping-pong

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　R4: last handover A -> B, failure 25 x 100 ms = 2.5 s after the handover (below Tstore_UE_cntxt = 5 s, so a recent handover), and re-establishment in C, which is neither the source A nor the target B: handover to wrong cell. Option 1 would need re-establishment in A. Option 3 would need no recent handover. Option 4 is not a failure class in TS 38.300 intra-system MRO.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.2.2 (UEInformationResponse field descriptions) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**062.** How is R5 classified?

1. Too late: 40.0 s exceeds the 5 s threshold; re-establishment was in cell C.
2. Handover to wrong cell, because C is neither A nor B.
3. Too early handover, because previousPCellId is present.
4. It cannot be classified, because timeConnFailure only has a resolution of 1 s.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　The detection rule compares the UE reported timer with the configured threshold (e.g. Tstore_UE_cntxt): 40.0 s > 5 s means no recent handover, and RLF with re-establishment in another cell is too late handover (from B towards C). Option 2 ignores the timer condition. Option 3 is wrong because the presence of previousPCellId alone does not make a handover recent. Option 4 is wrong because the resolution is 100 ms.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.2 (detection mechanism) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/); 3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.2.2 (timeConnFailure) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**063.** What does the RLF report field timeConnFailure indicate?

1. Elapsed time from connection failure to report submission, in seconds.
2. Elapsed time from failure to the next RRC connection, in seconds.
3. Time since handover execution, encoded in units of ten milliseconds.
4. Elapsed handover-to-failure time in 100 ms steps, saturating at 102.3 s.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　TS 38.331 defines timeConnFailure exactly so. Option 1 describes timeSinceFailure. Option 2 describes timeUntilReconnection. Option 3 has the wrong resolution.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.2.2 (UEInformationResponse field descriptions) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**064.** Is R6 caused by a coverage hole in cell A?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined from the table: R6 is not an intra-system mobility failure (no recent handover, and the UE came back in A itself), but to confirm a coverage hole the report's measResultLastServCell, measResultNeighCells and locationInfo (or MDT data) are needed.

**詳解**　Too late handover requires re-establishment in a different cell, which R6 lacks. A weak serving cell with no usable neighbour at a known location would support a coverage problem, which MRO is meant to distinguish from mobility problems.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clauses 15.5.2.1 and 15.5.2.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**065.** In the TS 38.300 MRO definitions, what does "successful handover" refer to?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　The UE state, namely the successful completion of the random access procedure in the target cell.

**詳解**　TS 38.300 clause 15.5.2.2.2 adds this clarification right after the three intra-system definitions.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**情境 015**　One RLF report in full

Telco-X (fictional) decoded an NR RLF report retrieved from a UE through UEInformationResponse. PLMN 001-01 is a test identity used in this example.

```
UEInformationResponse received 2026-11-23 08:15:02 in cell KLG-0230-2
nr-RLF-Report-r16 (decoded):
  measResultLastServCell-r16:   physCellId 77, SS-RSRP -121 dBm, SS-RSRQ -17.5 dB
  measResultNeighCells-r16 / measResultListNR-r16:
       physCellId 803   SS-RSRP -102 dBm
       physCellId 79    SS-RSRP -115 dBm
  c-RNTI-r16:                   0x4A1F
  previousPCellId-r16:          (absent)
  failedPCellId-r16:            cellGlobalId PLMN 001-01, cell KLG-0221-1 (PCI 77)
  reestablishmentCellId-r16:    PLMN 001-01, cell KLG-0230-2 (PCI 803)
  timeConnFailure-r16:          (absent)
  timeSinceFailure-r16:         74
  connectionFailureType-r16:    rlf
  rlf-Cause-r16:                t310-Expiry
  locationInfo-r16:             present
NCRT of KLG-0221-1: REL-79 (KLG-0221-3), REL-81 (KLG-0222-1); no relation for PCI 803
```

**071.** How does this report classify under the intra-system MRO definitions?

1. Too early handover
2. Handover to wrong cell
3. Too late handover
4. Handover failure

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　No recent handover (previousPCellId and timeConnFailure absent), RLF in KLG-0221-1, and re-establishment in a different cell (KLG-0230-2): too late handover. Options 1 and 2 require a recent handover. Option 4 contradicts connectionFailureType = rlf.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**072.** Assuming timeSinceFailure-r16 was set at the moment the UEInformationResponse was received (08:15:02), at what time did the connection failure occur?

1. 08:15:02
2. 08:13:48
3. 08:16:16
4. 08:14:28

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　timeSinceFailure is in seconds: 08:15:02 - 74 s = 08:15:02 - 1 min 14 s = 08:13:48. Option 1 is the retrieval time. Option 3 adds instead of subtracting. Option 4 subtracts only 34 s.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.2.2 (timeSinceFailure) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**073.** For how long does the UE keep its latest RLF report?

1. Until fetched, or 48 hours after connection failure.
2. For 24 hours after the failure.
3. Until the next RRC connection setup, whether or not it was fetched.
4. Indefinitely, until the network fetches it.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　TS 38.300 clause 15.5.2.2.1: the UE stores the latest RLF report until it is fetched by the network or for 48 hours after the connection failure is detected. Options 2 and 3 give wrong limits. Option 4 omits the 48-hour limit.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**074.** Can you conclude that the missing NCR from KLG-0221-1 to PCI 803 prevented a handover for this UE?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined from one report: whether the UE sent eventA3 reports for PCI 803 before the failure (trace) and the measurement configuration it had (excluded cells, offsets) are needed, plus enough similar reports to show a pattern.

**詳解**　The report shows PCI 803 19 dB stronger than the serving cell (-102 vs -121 dBm) and no relation for it, which supports a missing-neighbour hypothesis, but the UE might not have reported PCI 803 at all (for example if it was excluded).

**出處**　Curriculum 6.1.3; 3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**075.** What is the difference between connectionFailureType = hof and = rlf in an NR RLF report?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　hof means the connection failure was a handover failure, while rlf means it was a radio link failure.

**詳解**　TS 38.331 defines connectionFailureType as indicating whether the connection failure is due to radio link failure or handover failure; for a handover failure, failedPCellId indicates the target PCell of the failed handover.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.2.2 (connectionFailureType, failedPCellId) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

#### Reading TS 28.552 handover measurements

**情境 016**　A 15-minute PM report with intra- and inter-gNB relations

Telco-X (fictional) pulled one 15-minute performance measurement report for cell TXG-0450-1 after a spike in handover complaints.

```
PM report, gNB TXG-G0450, granularity period 15 min, 2026-11-24 19:00-19:15
measObjLdn = NRCellCU=TXG-0450-1, NRCellRelation=REL-0450-2   (target TXG-0450-2, same gNB)
    MM.HoExeIntraReq                    412
    MM.HoExeIntraSucc                   409
measObjLdn = NRCellCU=TXG-0450-1, NRCellRelation=REL-0461-1   (target TXG-0461-1, gNB TXG-G0461)
    MM.HoPrepInterReq                   288
    MM.HoPrepInterSucc                  286
    MM.HoExeInterReq                    286
    MM.HoExeInterSucc                   241
    MM.HoExeInterFail.RrcReestabReq     31
    MM.HoExeInterFail.RetrUeCtxtReq     9
    MM.HoExeInterFail.HoExeSupTimer     5
measObjLdn = NRCellCU=TXG-0450-1, NRCellRelation=REL-0472-3   (target TXG-0472-3, gNB TXG-G0472)
    MM.HoPrepInterReq                   97
    MM.HoPrepInterSucc                  96
    MM.HoExeInterReq                    96
    MM.HoExeInterSucc                   94
```

**076.** Which relation has the lowest handover execution success ratio, and what is it?

1. REL-0461-1, about 84.3%
2. REL-0450-2, about 99.3%
3. REL-0472-3, about 97.9%
4. REL-0461-1, about 99.3%

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　REL-0461-1: MM.HoExeInterSucc / MM.HoExeInterReq = 241 / 286 = 0.8427. REL-0450-2: MM.HoExeIntraSucc / MM.HoExeIntraReq = 409 / 412 = 0.9927. REL-0472-3: 94 / 96 = 0.9792. Option 2 and option 3 are the better relations. Option 4 is REL-0461-1's preparation success ratio (286 / 288 = 0.9931), not execution.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.1, 5.1.1.6.1.2, 5.1.1.6.1.7, 5.1.1.6.1.8, 5.1.1.6.2.1 and 5.1.1.6.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**077.** What do the 31 events in MM.HoExeInterFail.RrcReestabReq on REL-0461-1 mean?

1. UEs re-established in a cell of another gNB after the failed handover.
2. Source gNB receives RRCReestablishmentRequest with handoverFailure.
3. The handover execution supervision timer expired in the source gNB.
4. The AMF sent UE CONTEXT RELEASE COMMAND indicating an unsuccessful handover.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　TS 28.552 clause 5.1.1.6.1.9 lists event 2: reception of RrcReestablishmentRequest with cause handoverFailure from the UE in the source gNB, where the re-establishment occurred in the source gNB; this is consistent with, but on its own does not prove, a TS 38.300 too early handover (which requires re-establishment in the source cell). Option 1 is the RetrUeCtxtReq subcounter (re-establishment in another gNB). Option 3 is HoExeSupTimer. Option 4 is the UeCtxtRelCmd subcounter.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.6.1.9 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**078.** On which event is MM.HoExeInterSucc stepped?

1. On transmission of the RRCReconfiguration that triggers the inter-gNB handover.
2. On reception of RRCReconfigurationComplete at the target NRCellCU.
3. At the source, on UE CONTEXT RELEASE (Xn) or COMMAND (AMF).
4. On receipt of HANDOVER REQUEST ACKNOWLEDGE from the target.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　That is the collection method in TS 28.552 clause 5.1.1.6.1.8. Option 1 is MM.HoExeInterReq. Option 2 is the trigger of the intra-gNB counter MM.HoExeIntraSucc. Option 4 is MM.HoPrepInterSucc.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.2, 5.1.1.6.1.7, 5.1.1.6.1.8 and 5.1.1.6.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**079.** Why does TS 28.552 require duplicates to be filtered out in MM.HoExeInterFail?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Because one handover failure might cause more than one of the counted events (for example a re-establishment request and a supervision timer expiry), and it must be counted only once.

**詳解**　Clause 5.1.1.6.1.9 states: "As one handover failure might cause more than one of the above events, duplicates need to be filtered out." In this report the subcounters sum to 31 + 9 + 5 = 45 = 286 - 241, consistent with no double counting.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.6.1.9 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**080.** Did the 9 UEs counted in MM.HoExeInterFail.RetrUeCtxtReq re-establish in TXG-0472-3?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the subcounter only says that re-establishment occurred in another gNB; the cell identity is needed from the RLF reports (reestablishmentCellId) or from the XnAP RETRIEVE UE CONTEXT REQUEST records.

**詳解**　TS 28.552 counts reception of XnAP RETRIEVE UE CONTEXT REQUEST when the re-establishment occurred in another gNB, without naming the cell; it could be the target gNB or a third one.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.6.1.9 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); 3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 6.2.2 (reestablishmentCellId) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**情境 017**　Daily trend around an offset change

Harbor Wireless (fictional) charted a week of counters for one relation after a parameter change on 2026-11-19 at 02:00.

| Day | MM.HoExeInterReq | MM.HoExeInterSucc | HO.IntraSys.TooEarly | HO.IntraSys.TooLate | HO.IntraSys.ToWrongCell |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 11-16 | 3,120 | 3,071 | 4 | 10 | 1 |
| 11-17 | 3,205 | 3,150 | 5 | 12 | 2 |
| 11-18 | 3,098 | 3,046 | 3 | 11 | 1 |
| 11-19 | 3,688 | 3,402 | 71 | 2 | 6 |
| 11-20 | 3,742 | 3,421 | 80 | 1 | 7 |
| 11-21 | 3,701 | 3,395 | 77 | 2 | 5 |
| 11-22 | 3,690 | 3,390 | 74 | 1 | 6 |
| Relation | PNG-0310-1 -> PNG-0311-2 (REL-311), measured on NRCellCU PNG-0310-1 | | | | |
| Change | CHG-9120, 2026-11-19 02:00: eventA3 a3-Offset rsrp 6 -> 2 on PNG-0310-1 | | | | |

**081.** Which interpretation is most consistent with the trend?

1. Too late handovers increased after the change.
2. The earlier offset increased attempts and too-early events sharply.
3. Handover to wrong cell became the dominant failure class.
4. The change had no measurable effect.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Mean attempts before: (3,120 + 3,205 + 3,098) / 3 = 3,141; after: (3,688 + 3,742 + 3,701 + 3,690) / 4 = 3,705.25; increase 3,705.25 / 3,141 - 1 = 17.96%. Too early before: (4 + 5 + 3) / 3 = 4 per day; after: (71 + 80 + 77 + 74) / 4 = 75.5 per day. Option 1 is the opposite (too late fell from about 11 to about 1.5). Option 3 is wrong because wrong-cell counts (5 to 7) are far below too early. Option 4 ignores the clear step.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.25.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); 3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.5.2.2.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**082.** What is the execution success ratio on 11-20?

1. 98.3%
2. 92.3%
3. 8.6%
4. 91.4%

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　3,421 / 3,742 = 0.9142, about 91.4%. Option 1 is 11-17 or 11-18 (3,150 / 3,205 = 0.9828; 3,046 / 3,098 = 0.9832). Option 2 is 11-19 (3,402 / 3,688 = 0.9225). Option 3 is the failure share on 11-20 ((3,742 - 3,421) / 3,742 = 0.0858).

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**083.** How are HO.IntraSys.TooEarly, HO.IntraSys.TooLate and HO.IntraSys.ToWrongCell defined in TS 28.552?

1. Cumulative gNB-detected intra-system failure counters, per NRCellCU/NRCellRelation.
2. As gauges sampled once per granularity period, per gNB only.
3. As counters per EutranRelation for NR to E-UTRA mobility.
4. As counters incremented by the UE and reported in the RLF report.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　1

**詳解**　Clause 5.1.1.25.1: collection method CC, obtained by accumulating the failure events detected by the gNB during intra-system mobility, with measurement object class NRCellCU and NRCellRelation. Option 2 has the wrong collection method and object. Option 3 describes the inter-system counters. Option 4 is wrong because the gNB, not the UE, counts the events.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.25.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**084.** By how many dB did CHG-9120 lower the A3 entering threshold on PNG-0310-1?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　By 2 dB (a3-Offset from 6 x 0.5 = 3 dB to 2 x 0.5 = 1 dB; Off enters A3-1 directly, so the entering threshold falls by 3 - 1 = 2 dB).

**詳解**　All other terms of A3-1 are unchanged, so the entering threshold Mn - Mp > Off + Hys - (offsets) moves by exactly the change in Off.

**出處**　3GPP TS 38.331 V18.11.0 clause 5.5.4.4 and clause 6.3.2 (ReportConfigNR field description a3-Offset) (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.331/)

</details>

**085.** Did the handover performance of the reverse relation PNG-0311-2 -> PNG-0310-1 change as well?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the table covers only REL-311 measured on PNG-0310-1; the counters of the reverse NRCellRelation, measured on NRCellCU PNG-0311-2, are needed.

**詳解**　Neighbour cell relations are unidirectional and the measurements are collected per source NRCellCU and NRCellRelation, so the reverse direction has its own counters (and its own A3 configuration).

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.25.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); 3GPP TS 38.300 V18.11.0 clause 15.3.3.1 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.300/)

</details>

**情境 018**　NG-based handovers and preparation failure causes

Telco-X (fictional) has no Xn link between the two gNBs below, so handovers are prepared via the AMF. The OSS shows preparation failure subcounters per NGAP cause value.

```
NRCellCU ILN-0108-1, NRCellRelation REL-0120-2 (target ILN-0120-2, other gNB, no Xn: NG-based handover)
Period: 2026-11-23 00:00-24:00 (daily total)
MM.HoPrepInterReq                                                   1,560
MM.HoPrepInterSucc                                                  1,212
MM.HoPrepInterFail.cause (HANDOVER PREPARATION FAILURE from AMF), per cause value (TS 38.413 clause 9.3.1.2):
    no-radio-resources-available-in-target-cell                     301
    ho-target-not-allowed                                           41
    unknown-targetID                                                6
MM.HoExeInterReq                                                    1,212
MM.HoExeInterSucc                                                   1,187
```

**086.** What share of the requested handover preparations ended in a successful handover execution?

1. 77.7%
2. 97.9%
3. 76.1%
4. 22.3%

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　3

**詳解**　End-to-end: MM.HoExeInterSucc / MM.HoPrepInterReq = 1,187 / 1,560 = 0.7609, about 76.1%. Option 1 is the preparation success ratio (1,212 / 1,560 = 0.7769). Option 2 is the execution success ratio (1,187 / 1,212 = 0.9794). Option 4 is the preparation failure share (348 / 1,560 = 0.2231).

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.1, 5.1.1.6.1.2, 5.1.1.6.1.7 and 5.1.1.6.1.8 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**087.** On which event is MM.HoPrepInterReq stepped?

1. On transmission of the RRCReconfiguration that orders the handover.
2. On receipt of an eventA3 MeasurementReport from the UE.
3. On receipt of HANDOVER COMMAND from the AMF.
4. On HANDOVER REQUIRED (AMF) or HANDOVER REQUEST (Xn) transmission.

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　4

**詳解**　TS 28.552 clause 5.1.1.6.1.1 defines this collection method, covering both NG-based and Xn-based preparation. Option 1 is MM.HoExeInterReq. Option 2 is not a TS 28.552 trigger (a report does not always lead to a preparation). Option 3 is MM.HoPrepInterSucc for NG-based handover.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.1, 5.1.1.6.1.2 and 5.1.1.6.1.7 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**088.** Which preparation failure cause dominates, and what share of preparation failures does it represent?

1. ho-target-not-allowed, about 11.8%
2. no-radio-resources-available-in-target-cell, about 86.5%
3. unknown-targetID, about 1.7%
4. no-radio-resources-available-in-target-cell, about 19.3%

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　2

**詳解**　Failures: 301 + 41 + 6 = 348 (= 1,560 - 1,212). Share: 301 / 348 = 0.8649, about 86.5%. Option 1 is 41 / 348 = 0.1178. Option 3 is 6 / 348 = 0.0172. Option 4 divides by all requests (301 / 1,560 = 0.1929) instead of by the failures.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clause 5.1.1.6.1.3 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/); 3GPP TS 38.413 V18.11.0 clause 9.3.1.2 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/38_series/38.413/)

</details>

**089.** Is ILN-0120-2 short of radio resources all day or only at busy hour?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Cannot be determined: the data is a daily total; counters at 15-minute (or hourly) granularity and load measurements of ILN-0120-2 are needed.

**詳解**　A daily sum cannot show the time profile of the resource-related rejections.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.2 and 5.1.1.6.1.3 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

**090.** Why can the execution success ratio of a relation be high while its overall handover success is low?

<details><summary>看答案</summary>

**答案**　Because execution counting starts only when the RRCReconfiguration is sent after a successful preparation, so preparation failures (here 348) never enter the execution counters.

**詳解**　MM.HoExeInterReq is stepped on transmission of the RRCReconfiguration that triggers the handover, which happens only after preparation succeeded; a full picture needs both the preparation and execution counters.

**出處**　3GPP TS 28.552 V18.12.0 clauses 5.1.1.6.1.1 to 5.1.1.6.1.3 and 5.1.1.6.1.7 (https://www.3gpp.org/ftp/Specs/archive/28_series/28.552/)

</details>

#### MRO in operation: limits, coverage versus mobility, and special cases

**情境 019**　Automatic MRO changes against the OAM limits

Harbor Wireless (fictional) reviews the action log of its MRO function for one relation. In this vendor's implementation the MRO "Handover Trigger" is applied as the cellIndividualOffset of the target cell (fictional vendor mapping).

```
MRO action log, relation KHH-0805-1 -> KHH-0806-2 (REL-806)
OAM control parameters for MRO (fictional values):
  Maximum deviation of Handover Trigger:            3 dB from the default point of operation
  Minimum time between Handover Trigger changes:    24 h
  Default point of operation (set by OAM):          cellIndividualOffset rsrpOffsetSSB = dB0
  Tstore_UE_cntxt:                                  5 s
```
